Only when NO project command exists does it run a generic convergence loop — orient the canonical home, fan out the 15 named roles + the standing Long-Trail TDD case-owner, converge, adversarially review, verify, ship, reconcile. - **rvdna-call-variants**: rvDNA T0 — variant calls for a region in a pinned collection. Returns whatever variants the registered RVDNA file's VariantTensor section holds for the region. Empty array if no variant data registered (genuine answer, not a stub). Witness-anchored. ([ruvnet/RuLake](https://github.com/ruvnet/RuLake/blob/HEAD/plugins/rulake-rvdna/commands/rvdna-call-variants.md) · ★17)
- **rvdna-find**: rvDNA T0 — real brute-force L2 kNN over a pinned genomic collection. Returns a witnessed bundle pointer plus the matching segments. Deterministic; ties broken by lower id (matches the ADR-157 kernel-conformance contract). ([ruvnet/RuLake](https://github.com/ruvnet/RuLake/blob/HEAD/plugins/rulake-rvdna/commands/rvdna-find.md) · ★17)
- **rvdna-find**: rvDNA T0 — kNN search over a pinned genomic collection. Returns a witnessed bundle pointer plus the matching segments. v0.0.1 stub; real RaBitQ inner loop lands in v0.1. ([ruvnet/RuLake](https://github.com/ruvnet/RuLake/blob/HEAD/plugins/rulake-stack/commands/rvdna-find.md) · ★17)
- **rvdna-lineage**: rvDNA — internal capability tier. Reveals lineage / provenance metadata for a registered rvDNA collection (e.g. assembly version, source URI, build epoch). Gated by --capabilities internal. ([ruvnet/RuLake](https://github.com/ruvnet/RuLake/blob/HEAD/plugins/rulake-rvdna/commands/rvdna-lineage.md) · ★17)
- **rvdna-score**: rvDNA T0 — deterministic FNV-1a score in [0.0, 1.0] over (witness, region). Region-sensitive, witness-sensitive, reproducible across operators. Production scorer model wires here in v0.2. ([ruvnet/RuLake](https://github.com/ruvnet/RuLake/blob/HEAD/plugins/rulake-rvdna/commands/rvdna-score.md) · ★17)
- **rvdna-translate**: rvDNA T0 — real codon-table DNA → protein translation. Standard genetic code (64 codons → 20 amino acids + 3 stop codons). Case-insensitive, skips Ns + whitespace, stops at first stop codon. Witness-anchored. ([ruvnet/RuLake](https://github.com/ruvnet/RuLake/blob/HEAD/plugins/rulake-rvdna/commands/rvdna-translate.md) · ★17)
